==== r46-sal-mastercrafted ====
selectionEntry id=r46-sal-mastercrafted name=Master-crafted Weapon — Salamanders price type=upgrade hidden=false default=None cost=[('pts', '10')] constraints=[('r46-sal-mastercrafted-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Master-crafted Weapon — Salamanders price: A Salamanders model that may purchase a Master-crafted Weapon pays +10 points instead of +15. The selected weapon gains Master-crafted: re-roll one failed To Hit roll per turn. All normal eligibility restrictions remain.
  MODS 

==== r46-sal-artificer-armour ====
selectionEntry id=r46-sal-artificer-armour name=Artificer Armour — Salamanders access type=upgrade hidden=false default=None cost=[('pts', '15')] constraints=[('r46-sal-artificer-armour-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Artificer Armour — Salamanders access: A Salamanders non-Independent Character with Space Marine Armoury access may purchase Artificer Armour for +15 points even if its unit entry normally does not allow it. If the unit entry already offers Artificer Armour more cheaply, use that price instead. Artificer Armour grants a 2+ Armour Save.
  MODS

  RULE Heavy Flamer instead of Flamer: Where a Salamanders Tactical or Veteran Squad may purchase a Flamer, it may instead purchase a Heavy Flamer for +10 points. Use the Salamanders Heavy Flamer profile: Template, S6, AP4, Assault 1, Flame.

==== r46-sal-ceramite ====
selectionEntry id=r46-sal-ceramite name=Armoured Ceramite — Salamanders price type=upgrade hidden=false default=None cost=[('pts', '10')] constraints=[('r46-sal-ceramite-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Armoured Ceramite — Salamanders price: An eligible Salamanders Vehicle or Dreadnought pays +10 points for Armoured Ceramite instead of its normal price; Land Raiders and Spartans use their listed normal price. Armoured Ceramite removes the additional Armour Penetration die granted by Melta regardless of range.
  MODS 

==== r25-rite-xviii-0-the-covenant-of-fire ====
selectionEntry id=r25-rite-xviii-0-the-covenant-of-fire name=Salamanders Rite of War — The Covenant of Fire type=upgrade hidden=true default=None cost=[] constraints=[('r25-rite-xviii-0-the-covenant-of-fire-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Disciples of the Flame: Pyroclast Squads and Salamanders Infernus Destroyer Squads may be selected as non-compulsory Troops choices. They may not fulfil compulsory Troops selections.
  RULE Walk Through Fire: Salamanders Infantry units gain Move Through Cover.
  RULE Veneration of Wrath: When a Salamanders model makes an Armour Penetration roll with a Melta weapon, one individual Armour Penetration die that rolls a natural 1 may be re-rolled. The second result is accepted. This also applies to Melta Bombs.
  RULE Obsidian Forged: Each time a Salamanders Vehicle or Dreadnought suffers a Glancing or Penetrating Hit from a Flame, Melta, Plasma or Volkite attack, roll D6 before Vehicle Damage; on 5+ the hit is ignored. This also applies to Melta Bombs.
  RULE Limitations: Units may not deploy using Deep Strike. Units that must deploy by Deep Strike may not be selected. The combined number of Fast Attack and Heavy Support choices may not exceed the number of Troops choices. No Fortification.
  INFO Move Through Cover -> r36-shared-move-through-cover-b95132d type=rule
  MODS <ns0:modifiers xmlns:ns0="http://www.battlescribe.net/schema/catalogueSchema">

==== r25-rite-xviii-1-the-awakening-fire ====
selectionEntry id=r25-rite-xviii-1-the-awakening-fire name=Salamanders Rite of War — The Awakening Fire type=upgrade hidden=true default=None cost=[] constraints=[('r25-rite-xviii-1-the-awakening-fire-max', 'max', '1', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[]
  RULE Devils from the Dark: Salamanders Infantry units gain Fear.
  RULE Unto the Fires: Never Give Up functions normally. During the additional complete game turn generated by Never Give Up, all Salamanders Infantry units gain Fearless.
  RULE Fury of the Salamander: Salamanders Librarians may select Fury of the Salamander as a Pyromancy power: Range 18", Strength 5, AP1, Witchfire — Beam, Assault 1. Elemental Horror: if an enemy unit suffers one or more unsaved Wounds, it immediately takes a Morale test with a Leadership penalty equal to those unsaved Wounds.
  RULE Limitations: The Detachment must include a Legion Chaplain Consul. It may include no more than one unit of each type: Jump Infantry, Jetbike, Skimmer and Flyer. Vulkan may not be included. No Fortification or Allied Detachment.
  INFO Fear -> r36-shared-fear-689e027 type=rule
  INFO Fearless -> r36-shared-fearless-8699dbc type=rule
  INFO Fury of the Salamander -> r40-sal-prof-fury type=profile

==== r41-unit-xviii-5-artellus-numeon ====

  RULE Wargear: Artificer Armour, Iron Halo, Master-crafted Thunder Hammer, Bolt Pistol, Frag Grenades, Salamanders Mantle.
  RULE Captain of the Pyre Guard: Numeon may select one Firedrake Terminator Squad as his personal retinue. One Firedrake in that unit may be upgraded to a Firedrake Master at no additional points cost. The squad occupies no separate Elites selection.
  RULE Vulkan's Heir: Numeon and any Salamanders unit he has joined may re-roll failed Morale tests.
  RULE Command Retinue: Instead of Firedrakes, Numeon may select a Legion Command Squad or Legion Terminator Command Squad as his retinue.

==== r40-sal-nomus-contemptor-hq ====
selectionEntry id=r40-sal-nomus-contemptor-hq name=Legion Contemptor Dreadnought — Keeper of the Keys type=unit hidden=true default=None cost=[('pts', '155')] constraints=[] cats=[('r40-sal-nomus-contemptor-hq-copy-contemptor-unit-cat', 'Elites', 'cat-elites', 'false'), ('r40-sal-nomus-contemptor-hq-cat-cat-hq', 'HQ', 'cat-hq', 'true'), ('r40-sal-nomus-contemptor-hq-lim', 'Nomus — Keeper of the Keys', 'r40-sal-nomus-dread-hq', None)]
  RULE Standard Wargear: Twin-linked Heavy Bolter; Dreadnought Close Combat Weapon with built-in Twin-linked Bolter; Smoke Launchers; Searchlight. |  | Twin-Linked: Re-roll failed To Hit rolls with this weapon. Does not count as two weapons. Twin-linked Template weapons automatically hit all models fully or partially under the Template.
  RULE Atomantic Shielding: Each time the vehicle suffers a Glancing or Penetrating Hit from shooting, roll a D6; on a 5+ the hit is ignored. Against a Glancing or Penetrating Hit in close combat, the hit is ignored on a 6+. Make this roll before the Vehicle Damage table. If the vehicle suffers an Explodes! result, add +1" to the explosion radius.
  RULE Paired Close-Combat Arms: If equipped with two Dreadnought Close Combat Weapons, the Contemptor gains +1 Attack. This bonus is not included in the profile.
  RULE Keeper of the Keys: Selected through Nomus Rhy'tan. This is a non-compulsory HQ choice and may not be the army's Warlord.

selectionEntry id=hq-praetor name=Legion Praetor type=unit hidden=false default=None cost=[('pts', '125')] constraints=[('hq-praetor-max', 'max', '2', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[('hq-praetor-foc-cat', 'HQ', 'cat-hq', 'true'), ('r41-praetor-iron-halo-cat', 'Iron Halo — army-wide limit', 'r41-iron-halo-army-limit', None)]
  RULE Standard Wargear: Power Armour, Bolt Pistol, Chainsword, Frag Grenades and Iron Halo. The Iron Halo grants a 4+ Invulnerable Save and is already included in the 125-point cost.
  RULE Legiones Astartes: The model has the Legiones Astartes special rule, also has And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic, and is not Fearless unless another rule says otherwise. |  | And They Shall Know No Fear: Automatically passes Regroup tests at the start of the turn and may be used normally for the rest of the turn. If caught by a pursuit, the unit automatically regroups and is re-engaged in close combat, and does not suffer extra wounds from being caught in a pursuit. Immune to Fear. | Fear: Enemy units in base contact must pass a Leadership test before resolving their close combat attacks. If
  RULE Frag Grenades: Uses the normal ProHammer Frag Grenade and Assault Grenade rules. Against vehicles, Frag Grenades have Strength 4 + D6 Armour Penetration.
  RULE Space Marine Armoury: The Praetor may select up to 100 points of weapons and wargear from the Space Marine Armoury. Armour, Mobility, Weapon Replacements and Additional Wargear all count toward the same 100-point limit. All normal Armoury restrictions apply.
  RULE Sorcerers of Prospero — Praetor: In a Thousand Sons Detachment, a Legion Praetor is a Psyker (Mastery Level 2). It selects its psychic powers from the single Psychic Discipline associated with its chosen Prosperine Cult: Pavoni — Biomancy; Raptora — Telekinesis; Corvidae — Divination; Athanaeans — Telepathy; Pyrae — Pyromancy, unless another rule explicitly states otherwise. Activating a Force Weapon counts as the use of a psychic power, and the same power may not be used more than once in the same player turn.

==== hq-centurion ====
selectionEntry id=hq-centurion name=Legion Centurion type=unit hidden=false default=None cost=[('pts', '50')] constraints=[('hq-centurion-max', 'max', '2', 'parent', 'selections')] cats=[('hq-centurion-foc-cat', 'HQ', 'cat-hq', 'true')]
  RULE Standard Wargear: Power Armour, Bolt Pistol, Chainsword and Frag Grenades.
  RULE Legiones Astartes: The model has the Legiones Astartes special rule, also has And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic, and is not Fearless unless another rule says otherwise. |  | And They Shall Know No Fear: Automatically passes Regroup tests at the start of the turn and may be used normally for the rest of the turn. If caught by a pursuit, the unit automatically regroups and is re-engaged in close combat, and does not suffer extra wounds from being caught in a pursuit. Immune to Fear. | Fear: Enemy units in base contact must pass a Leadership test before resolving their close combat attacks. If
  RULE Frag Grenades: Uses the normal ProHammer Frag Grenade and Assault Grenade rules. Against vehicles, Frag Grenades have Strength 4 + D6 Armour Penetration.
  RULE Legion Consuls: A Legion Centurion may be upgraded to no more than one Consul type. A Consul retains the Centurion profile, wargear, special rules and Armoury access unless stated otherwise. Wargear granted by a Consul is included in its upgrade cost and does not count toward the Armoury allowance. Consul restrictions override normal Centurion options.
  RULE Space Marine Armoury: The Centurion may select up to 100 points of weapons and wargear from the Space Marine Armoury. Armour, Mobility, Weapon Replacements and Additional Wargear all count toward the same 100-point limit. All normal Armoury restrictions apply. Consul-granted wargear is included in the Consul cost and does not count against this allowance.
  RULE Sorcerers of Prospero — Independent Character: In a Thousand Sons Detachment, a Legion Centurion and its Consul variants are Psykers (Mastery Level 1) unless another rule grants a higher Mastery Level. Its psychic powers are selected from the single Psychic Discipline associated with its chosen Prosperine Cult: Pavoni — Biomancy; Raptora — Telekinesis; Corvidae — Divination; Athanaeans — Telepathy; Pyrae — Pyromancy. A Librarian Consul follows the normal Librarian upgrade rules; an Epistolary is Mastery Level 2.

  RULE Honour of the Legion: The Chaplain and any unit he has joined have the Fearless special rule. |  | Fearless: Automatically passes all Morale and Pinning tests. May not Take Cover! or choose to voluntarily fail a break test.
  RULE Liturgies of Battle: In a player turn in which the Chaplain and a unit he has joined successfully charge, the Chaplain and all models in that unit may re-roll failed To Hit rolls in close combat for that Assault phase.
